# What the vendor's video courses teach, and whether Cut Craft can do it

Generated by `scripts/courses-report.py` from the course captions that `scripts/courses`
fetches into `re/courses/` (gitignored) and the hand-checked capability table
`docs/courses-capabilities.tsv`. Edit the table, then run both scripts again.

39 courses, 689 course entries, 449 distinct videos. Of 139 capabilities: 98 yes, 27 partly, 10 no, 4 out of scope (laser, the vendor's online services, file formats and gadget API, release overviews).

A video *teaches* a capability when its title names it; videos whose titles name none
(projects such as signs and plaques) list the three capabilities their captions lean on
most. *Videos* counts every video whose captions match a capability's pattern.

## Gaps, most used first

| Rank | Capability | Cut Craft | Videos | Issue |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Import Component / 3D Model: STL / OBJ / clipart; orientation, size, units, depth, discard below zero | partly: STL, OBJ (v / f, polygons fanned) and own .ccclip through Orientate 3D Model: orientation, rotation in quarter turns, size, units, depth, discard below zero, undercuts shown, both sides in a double-sided job; no on-screen rotation handles | 43 | #67 |
| 2 | Segment 3D Model: several cuts, segment tree, layout orientations, undercut highlight | partly: Segment 3D Model: one plane at a percentage, or several cuts in equal slices along X, Y or Z; segments after the first can be turned over; laid out in a row; a segment that still has undercuts says so. No segment tree (cutting one segment again) or per-segment layout orientation | 17 | #85 |
| 3 | Create Shape: custom profile, limit %, scale, total height, preserve internal corners, ring between two vectors, blend to inner vectors, live 3D handles, E to edit | partly: Create Shape: round, angular, smooth, flat or custom (an open vector's profile) over closed vectors, rings between nested vectors, limit, base, Scale (profile to a height), Total Height (with the base), combine. | Import: Highlight Undercuts and Create Both Sides (front and back components) | yes | Orientate 3D Model: Show Undercuts colours the faces a cutter from above cannot reach while the form is open; a full model in a double-sided job puts its underside on the other side (Create Both Sides) | 28 |
| Components right-click Re-Import (back to the import form) | partly | the component menu has Re-import Model | 8 |
| Export STL (tolerance, triangle count, back face), greyscale bitmap, 3D clipart | partly | Export as STL / Greyscale Bitmap / 3D Clipart; no tolerance, triangle limit or back face options, no OBJ | 12 |

| Segment 3D Model: several cuts, segment tree, layout orientations, undercut highlight | partly | Segment 3D Model: one plane at a percentage, or several cuts in equal slices along X, Y or Z; segments after the first can be turned over; laid out in a row; a segment that still has undercuts says so. No segment tree (cutting one segment again) or per-segment layout orientation | 17 |
| Slice Model: by thickness, count, custom slice list | yes | Slice Model: by thickness from the top or bottom, by number of slices, or a Custom Slice List of each slice's thickness; optional boundary vectors | 17 |

| Rotary jobs: wrapped toolpaths, posts wrapping onto A, 3D view wrapping | yes | rotary Job Setup, Auto-Wrapping View, wrapped posts | 29 |
| Rotary gadgets: fluting layout, spiral layout | yes | wrapped-fluting-layout, wrapped-spiral-layout | 8 |
| Rounding toolpath for a square or round blank | yes | rounding-toolpath command | 30 |
| Rotary import: full / flat model, scale to fit, move the rotation axis, interactive rotation | partly | full / flat model and scale to fit; no rotation handles, no moving the axis | 10 |
| Vector Unwrapper (rotary) | yes | vector-unwrapper command | 17 |
| Celtic Weave Creator gadget | yes | celtic-weave command | 1 |
